# POD Personal Character Interactions
#  Shapeshift

POD_vampshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_vampire
	#interface_priority = 92
	#icon = "gfx/interface/icons/character_interactions/blood_surge_interaction.dds"
	desc = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_desc

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = { has_POD_vamp_blood_trigger = yes }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = ritual_stone_of_the_true_form_modifier }
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
		}
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = malleable_visage_perk
					has_trait = vicissitudeadvanced
				}
			}
		}
		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_vicissitudeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				OR = {
					has_perk = malleable_visage_perk
					has_trait = vicissitudeadvanced
				}
			}
		}
		flag = revertvicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_revertvicissitudeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = malleable_visage_perk
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				is_immortal = yes
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = feral_weapons_perk
					has_trait = proteanadvanced
					#has_trait = serpentisadvanced
					has_perk = rend_the_osseous_frame_perk
					has_trait = vicissitudeadvanced
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_feralweapons_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = feralweapons
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_feralweapons
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = eyes_of_the_beast_perk
					has_trait = proteanadvanced
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_beasteyes_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = beasteyes
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_beasteyes
	}

	send_option = {
	    is_shown = {
		    scope:actor = {
			    OR = {
			    is_setite_trigger = yes
				    has_perk = serpent_eyes_perk
					has_trait = serpentisadvanced
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_serpenteyes_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = serpenteyes
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_serpenteyes_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = rend_the_osseous_frame_perk
					has_trait = vicissitudeadvanced
					has_perk = bone_obedience_perk
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_bonearmor_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = bonearmor
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_bonearmor
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = shapechange_perk
					has_trait = proteanadvanced
					has_perk = predators_transformation_perk
					has_trait = abombweadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = wolf
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_wolf
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = shapechange_perk
					has_trait = proteanadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = boar
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_boar
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = shapechange_perk
					has_trait = proteanadvanced
					has_perk = predators_transformation_perk
					has_trait = abombweadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = lion
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_lion
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
				    has_perk = form_of_the_snake_perk
					has_trait = serpentisadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = giantserpent
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_giantserpent
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = metamorphosis_perk
					has_trait = proteanadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = rat
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_rat
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = metamorphosis_perk

				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no

	send_option = {

	send_option = {

			}

	send_option = {

				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = mist
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_mist
	}

	send_option = {
	    is_shown = {
		    scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = eyes_of_the_abyss_perk
					has_trait = obtenebrationadvanced
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_abysseyes_modifier }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_darksight_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = abysseyes
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_abysseyes
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = tenebrous_avatar_perk
					has_trait = obtenebrationadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = shadow
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_shadow
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
				has_trait_with_flag = unlock_incorporeal_form # Blood Sorcery Advanced
			}
		}
		flag = incorporeal
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_incorporeal
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = chiropteran_marauder_perk
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = chiropteran
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_chiropteran
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {

		}
		flag = zulo

	send_option = {

    send_option = {

		}
		flag = serpentsflesh
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_serpentsflesh
	}

    send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
				    has_perk = typhonic_avatar_perk
					has_trait = serpentisadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = typhonicavatar
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_typhonicavatar
	}

    send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = godhead_perk
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = godhead
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_godhead
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = incarnate_darkness_perk
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = incarnatedarkness
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_incarnate_darkness
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_perk = skinofchameleon_perk
					has_trait = visceratikaadvanced
				}
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = skinofchameleon
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_skinofchameleon
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = aspect_of_the_beast_perk
				has_aspect_of_the_beast_trigger = no
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_beavers_bite
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_chameeleons_colors
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_ears_of_the_hare
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_falcons_eye
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_ferocity_of_the_cougar
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_leapfrog
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_nose_of_the_hound
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_serpents_venom
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_sound_of_the_cricket
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_squirrels_balance
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_strength_of_the_bear
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_swiftness_of_the_stag
				}
			}
		}
		flag = aspect_of_the_beast
		localization = aspect_of_the_beast_perk_name
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = aspect_of_the_beast_perk
				NAND = {
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_beavers_bite
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_chameeleons_colors
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_ears_of_the_hare
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_falcons_eye
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_ferocity_of_the_cougar
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_leapfrog
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_nose_of_the_hound
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_serpents_venom
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_sound_of_the_cricket
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_squirrels_balance
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_strength_of_the_bear
					has_character_flag = aspect_of_the_beast_swiftness_of_the_stag
				}
			}
		}
		flag = purchase_aspect_of_the_beast
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_purchase_aspect_of_the_beast
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = the_wild_beast_perk
				has_POD_nonhumanform_trigger = no
			}
		}
		flag = the_wild_beast
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_the_wild_beast
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mythic_form_perk
			}
		}
		flag = phoenix
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_phoenix_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mythic_form_perk
			}
		}
		flag = unicorn

	send_option = {

			}

	send_option = {

	send_option = {

	send_option = {

				has_perk = elemental_form_perk

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = elemental_form_perk
			}
		}
		flag = wood
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_wood_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_aspect_of_the_beast_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:humanoid = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_minimal_chance_of_reducing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_moderate_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				POD_minimal_chance_of_reducing_masquerade_exposure = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:immortal_age = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701
			}
		}
		# Ruler Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:godhead = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_godhead_modifier
				}
				POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
				POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
			}
		}
		# Commander Shapeshift #
		if ={
		    limit = {
			    scope:serpenteyes = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
			    add_character_modifier = {
				    modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_serpenteyes_modifier
				}
				POD_small_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:feralweapons = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_dread = 10
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_feralweapons_modifier
				}
				POD_minimal_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:bonearmor = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_dread = 10
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_bonearmor_modifier
				}
				POD_moderate_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:wolf = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_wolf_modifier
				}
				POD_small_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:boar = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_boar_modifier
				}
				POD_small_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lion = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_lion_modifier
				}
				POD_small_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:zulo = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_dread = 40
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_zulo_modifier
				}
				POD_moderate_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		# Medium Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:the_wild_beast = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_the_wild_beast_modifier
				}
				POD_moderate_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:chiropteran = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_dread = 60
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_chiropteran_modifier
				}
				POD_very_high_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure  = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:serpentsflesh = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				if = {
				    limit = {
					    OR = {
						    has_perk = smooth_scales_perk
							has_trait = serpentisadvanced
						}
					}
                    add_character_modifier = {
					    modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_smooth_scales_modifier
					}
				}
				else = {
				    add_character_modifier = {
					    modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_serpents_flesh_modifier
					}
				}
				POD_moderate_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:typhonicavatar = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
			    if = {
				    limit = {
					    has_perk = apophis_perk
					}
					add_dread = 30
					add_character_modifier = {
					    modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_apophis_modifier
					}
				}
				else_if = {
				    limit = {
					    OR = {
						    has_perk = form_of_the_storm_perk
							has_trait = serpentisadvanced
						}
					}
					add_dread = 20
                    add_character_modifier = {
					    modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_form_of_the_storm_modifier
					}
				}
				else = {
				    add_dread = 10
				    add_character_modifier = {
					    modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_typhonic_avatar_modifier
					}
				}
				POD_high_risk_of_increasing_masquerade_exposure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:incarnatedarkness = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {

# Mummy Shapeshift
POD_mummy_shapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_mummy
	desc = POD_mummy_shapeshift_interaction_desc
	icon = POD_hekau_interaction

	cooldown = { months = 1 }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = { has_trait = mummy }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:actor = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { is_east_mummy_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = yangchi0_modifier }
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { is_amenti_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = sekhem0_modifier }
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = ritual_stone_of_the_true_form_modifier }
		}
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = whispers_to_the_body_perk
			}
		}
		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_reshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks

			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age

			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
		}
		if = {

		# Vicissitude Gui #

			}

			limit = {
				scope:mercury = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mercury_modifier

			}
		}
		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #

			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_tin_modifier

# Werewolf Shapeshift
POD_wereshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_werewolf_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = werewolf
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = werewolf_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {

							has_trait = abomination

						}
						has_character_flag = werewolf_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_werewolf_form_crinos_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = crinos
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_crinos

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_werewolf_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:homid = yes
						scope:crinos = yes
						scope:glabro = yes
						scope:hispo = yes
						scope:lupus = yes
					}
				}
				POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes

					modifier = POD_werewolf_form_crinos_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:glabro = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {

					modifier = POD_werewolf_form_glabro_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:hispo = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_werewolf_form_hispo_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lupus = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_werewolf_form_lupus_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# Commander Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:greendragon = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_greendragon_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# Ruler Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:hakaken = yes
			}

				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:glouogh = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_glouogh_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:darkfungus = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_darkfungus_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# Diplomacy Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:pharaoh = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_pharaoh_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# Low Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:giantserpent = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_giantserpent_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_rat_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:bat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_bat_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:insect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_insect_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:snake = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_snake_modifier
			}
		}
		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:incorporeal = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_incorporeal_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mist = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mist_modifier
			}
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:wolf = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_wolf_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:boar = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_boar_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lion = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_lion_modifier
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
	}

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = ajaba
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = ajaba_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}


	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = ajaba
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = ajaba_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_ajaba_form_crinos_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = crinos
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_crinos
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {

					AND = {

					AND = {
						OR = {

						}

				}

			}
		}
		flag = crocas
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_crocas
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = ajaba
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = ajaba_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_ajaba_form_hyaenid_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = hyaenid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_hyaenid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mooncat_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = mooncat
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_ajaba_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:homid = yes
						scope:crinos = yes
						scope:anthros = yes
						scope:crocas = yes
						scope:hyaenid = yes
					}
				}
				POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:crinos = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ajaba_form_crinos_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:anthros = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ajaba_form_anthros_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:crocas = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ajaba_form_crocas_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:hyaenid = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ajaba_form_hyaenid_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {

			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_insect
	}

	send_option = {

				has_perk = all_beasts_under_the_sun_perk
			}
		}
		flag = snake
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_snake
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_bastet_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:homid = yes
						scope:crinos = yes
						scope:sokto = yes
						scope:chatro = yes
						scope:feline = yes
					}
				}
				POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:immortal_age = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:crinos = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bastet_form_crinos_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:sokto = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bastet_form_sokto_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:chatro = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bastet_form_chatro_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:feline = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bastet_form_feline_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# Low Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:giantserpent = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_giantserpent_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_rat_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:bat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_bat_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:insect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_insect_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:snake = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_snake_modifier
			}
		}
		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:cat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = { POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes }
			scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_cat_modifier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = { POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes }
			scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier }
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:wolf = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_wolf_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:boar = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_boar_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lion = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_lion_modifier
			}
		}
		# Medium Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:water = yes
			}
			scope:actor = { POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes }
			scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_water_modifier }
		}
		# High Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:shiva = yes
			}
			scope:actor = { POD_rage_decrease_multiple_effect = { COUNT = 2 } }
			scope:actor = { POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 } }
			scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_shiva_modifier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:kali = yes
			}
			scope:actor = { POD_rage_decrease_multiple_effect = { COUNT = 2 } }
			scope:actor = { POD_gift_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 } }
			scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_kali_modifier }
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
	}
}

POD_coraxshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_corax_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = corax
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = corax_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}


	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = corax
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = corax_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_corax_form_crinos_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = crinos
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_crinos
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = corax
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = corax_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_corax_form_corvid_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = corvid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_corvid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mooncat_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = mooncat
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_corax_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:homid = yes
						scope:crinos = yes
						scope:corvid = yes
					}
				}
				POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:crinos = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_corax_form_crinos_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:corvid = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_corax_form_corvid_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		#modifier = {
		#	add = 60
		#	scope:crinos = yes
		#	is_knight = yes
		#	OR = {
		#		is_at_war = yes
		#		trigger_if = {
		#			limit = { is_independent_ruler = no }
		#			liege = { is_at_war = yes }
		#		}
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 40
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:corvid = yes
		#		scope:mooncat = yes
		#	}
		#	is_at_war = no
		#	OR = {
		#		any_scheme = { scheme_skill = intrigue }
		#		has_council_position = councillor_spymaster
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:corvid = yes
		#	is_in_army = no
		#	has_trait = beast_born
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:homid = yes

		#	NOT = { any_scheme = { exists = yes } }
		#	NOT = { any_targeting_scheme = { hostile_scheme_trigger = yes } }

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:kyubi = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_kitsune_form_kyubi_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:immortal_age = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701
			}
		}
		# Medium Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:water = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_water_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:fire = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_fire_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:earth = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_earth_modifier
			}
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:wolf = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_wolf_modifier

			}

		if = {

					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_boar_modifier

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {

			scope:actor = {

POD_mokoleshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_mokole_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = mokole
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = mokole_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}


	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = mokole
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = mokole_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_archid_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = archid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_archid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = mokole
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = mokole_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = suchid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_suchid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				always = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_reptoid_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = reptoid

	}

	send_option = {

		}
		flag = bat

				has_perk = steal_shape_perk
			}
		}
		flag = insect

	}

	send_option = {

			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = steal_shape_perk
			}
		}
		flag = snake
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_snake
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_mokole_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:homid = yes
						scope:archid = yes
						scope:reptoid = yes
						scope:suchid = yes
						scope:drachid = yes
					}
				}
				POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:reptoid = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_mokole_form_reptoid_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:archid = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_mokole_form_archid_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {

				scope:suchid = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:drachid = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_mokole_form_drachid_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# Low Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:giantserpent = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_giantserpent_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {

			}

		if = {

			}
			scope:actor = {

			}
		}
		if = {

			}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {

			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier

					AND = {

	send_option = {

	send_option = {

			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = there_is_no_body_perk
			}
		}
		flag = incorporeal
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_incorporeal
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_nagah_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = form_mastery_perk } }
				POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							scope:homid = yes
							scope:azhi_dahaka = yes
							scope:silkaram = yes
							scope:kali_dahaka = yes
							scope:vasuki = yes
						}
					}
					POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes

					POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
				}

			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:azhi_dahaka = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_nagah_form_azhi_dahaka_modifier
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_perk = mahanaga_perk }
					add_character_modifier = mahanaga_perk_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:silkaram = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_nagah_form_silkaram_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:kali_dahaka = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_nagah_form_kali_dahaka_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vasuki = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_nagah_form_vasuki_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:incorporeal = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_incorporeal_modifier
			}
		}
	}

		base = 0
	}
}

POD_ratkinshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_ratkin_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = ratkin
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = ratkin_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}


	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

			}
		}
		flag = ratman
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_ratman
	}

	send_option = {

			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = beast_born
				culture = { has_cultural_era_or_later = culture_era_final_nights } # TV
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_ratkin_form_itchy_modifier  }
			}
		}
		is_valid = {

		flag = itchy

	send_option = {

			}

		}
		flag = dragon
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_dragon_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = legendary_form_perk
			}
		}
		flag = phoenix
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_phoenix_modifier

	send_option = {

				has_perk = legendary_form_perk
			}
		}
		flag = griffin
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_griffin_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = treppelganger_perk
			}
		}
		flag = treppelganger
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_treppelganger_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_ratkin_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:homid = yes
						scope:ratman = yes
						scope:crinos = yes
						scope:rodens = yes
						scope:itchy = yes

						scope:dragon = yes
						scope:phoenix = yes
						scope:unicorn = yes
						scope:griffin = yes
					}
				}
				POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:ratman = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ratkin_form_ratman_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:crinos = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ratkin_form_crinos_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rodens = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ratkin_form_rodens_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:itchy = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ratkin_form_itchy_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:treppelganger = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_treppelganger_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# High Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:dragon = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_stress = 50
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_dragon_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# War Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:phoenix = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_stress = 50
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_phoenix_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:unicorn = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_stress = 50
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_unicorn_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:griffin = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_stress = 50
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_griffin_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
	}
}

POD_apisshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_apis_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = apis
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = apis_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}


	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = apis
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = apis_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_apis_form_crinos_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = crinos
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_crinos
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = apis
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = apis_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_apis_form_aurochs_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = aurochs
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_aurochs
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mooncat_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = mooncat
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_apis_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:homid = yes
						scope:crinos = yes
						scope:aurochs = yes
					}
				}
				POD_rage_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:crinos = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_apis_form_crinos_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:aurochs = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_apis_form_aurochs_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		#modifier = {
		#	add = 60
		#	scope:crinos = yes
		#	is_knight = yes
		#	OR = {
		#		is_at_war = yes
		#		trigger_if = {
		#			limit = { is_independent_ruler = no }
		#			liege = { is_at_war = yes }
		#		}
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 40
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:aurochs = yes
		#		scope:mooncat = yes
		#	}
		#	is_at_war = no
		#	OR = {
		#		any_scheme = { scheme_skill = intrigue }
		#		has_council_position = councillor_spymaster
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:aurochs = yes
		#	is_in_army = no
		#	has_trait = beast_born
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:homid = yes
		#	has_trait = homid_born
		#	NOT = { any_scheme = { exists = yes } }
		#	NOT = { any_targeting_scheme = { hostile_scheme_trigger = yes } }
		#	is_at_war = no
		#}
	}
}

# Etomos Shapeshift
POD_ananasishapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_ananasi_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = ananasi
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = ananasi_shape
				}
			}
		}

		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = ananasi
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = ananasi_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_ananasi_form_warform_modifier }
			}

		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_warform

	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_ananasi_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:warform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ananasi_form_warform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:macroform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_ananasi_form_macroform_modifier
				}

				scope:microform = yes
			}

					modifier = POD_ananasi_form_microform_modifier
				}

					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

POD_bahaeshshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_ananasi_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = bahaesh
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = bahaesh_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = bahaesh
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = bahaesh_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_warform_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = warform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_warform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = bahaesh
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = bahaesh_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_macroform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = macroform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_macroform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = bahaesh
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = bahaesh_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_microform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = microform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_microform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				always = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_swarmform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = swarmform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_swarmform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = titan_born
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_titaniform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = titaniform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_titaniform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mooncat_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = mooncat
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_bahaesh_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:warform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_warform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:macroform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_macroform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:microform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_microform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:titaniform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_titaniform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:swarmform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_bahaesh_form_swarmform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		#modifier = {
		#	add = 60
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:warform = yes
		#		scope:titaniform = yes
		#	}
		#	is_knight = yes
		#	OR = {
		#		is_at_war = yes
		#		trigger_if = {
		#			limit = { is_independent_ruler = no }
		#			liege = { is_at_war = yes }
		#		}
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 20
		#	scope:macroform = yes
		#	is_knight = yes
		#	OR = {
		#		is_at_war = yes
		#		trigger_if = {
		#			limit = { is_independent_ruler = no }
		#			liege = { is_at_war = yes }
		#		}
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 20
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:titaniform = yes
		#	}
		#	OR = {
		#		is_commanding_army = yes
		#		has_council_position = councillor_marshal
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 40
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:microform = yes
		#		scope:mooncat = yes
		#	}
		#	is_at_war = no
		#	OR = {
		#		any_scheme = { scheme_skill = intrigue }
		#		has_council_position = councillor_spymaster
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 20
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:macroform = yes
		#	}
		#	is_at_war = no
		#	OR = {
		#		any_scheme = { scheme_skill = intrigue }
		#		has_council_position = councillor_spymaster
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:microform = yes
		#	is_in_army = no
		#	has_trait = micro_born
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:macroform = yes
		#	is_in_army = no
		#	has_trait = macro_born
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:titaniform = yes
		#	is_in_army = no

POD_umakhishapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_ananasi_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = umakhi
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = umakhi_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = umakhi
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = umakhi_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umakhi_form_warform_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = warform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_warform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = umakhi
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = umakhi_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umakhi_form_macroform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = macroform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_macroform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = umakhi
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = umakhi_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umakhi_form_microform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = microform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_microform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				always = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umakhi_form_swarmform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = swarmform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_swarmform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = titan_born
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umakhi_form_titaniform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = titaniform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_titaniform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mooncat_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = mooncat
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_umakhi_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:warform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umakhi_form_warform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:macroform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umakhi_form_macroform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:microform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umakhi_form_microform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:titaniform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umakhi_form_titaniform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:swarmform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umakhi_form_swarmform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		#modifier = {
		#	add = 60
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:warform = yes
		#		scope:titaniform = yes
		#	}

		#		trigger_if = {
		#			limit = { is_independent_ruler = no }
		#			liege = { is_at_war = yes }
		#		}
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 20
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:titaniform = yes
		#	}
		#	OR = {

		#

					has_trait = umbulali
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = umbulali_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umbulali_form_macroform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = macroform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_macroform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = umbulali
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = umbulali_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umbulali_form_microform_modifier  }

		flag = microform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_microform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				always = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umbulali_form_swarmform_modifier  }
			}

	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = titan_born
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_umbulali_form_titaniform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = titaniform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_titaniform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mooncat_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = mooncat
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_umbulali_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:warform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umbulali_form_warform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:macroform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umbulali_form_macroform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:microform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_umbulali_form_microform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:titaniform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {

					modifier = POD_umbulali_form_titaniform_modifier

			}

			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

POD_umhlolishapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_ananasi_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = umhloli
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = umhloli_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {

POD_kstywshapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_werewolf
	interface_priority = 92
	common_interaction = yes
	icon = POD_ananasi_interaction
	desc = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = kstyw
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = abomination
						has_trait = spirit
					}
					has_character_flag = kstyw_shape
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = homid
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_homid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = kstyw
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = kstyw_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_kstyw_form_warform_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = warform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_warform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = kstyw
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = kstyw_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_kstyw_form_macroform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = macroform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_macroform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = kstyw
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_trait = abomination
							has_trait = spirit
						}
						has_character_flag = kstyw_shape
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_kstyw_form_microform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = microform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_microform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				always = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_kstyw_form_swarmform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = swarmform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_swarmform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_trait = titan_born
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_kstyw_form_titaniform_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = titaniform
		localization = POD_wereshapeshift_interaction_titaniform
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = mooncat_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = mooncat
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
	}

	send_options_exclusive = yes

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_kstyw_form_effect = yes
		}
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			POD_gnosis_loss_check_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:homid = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:warform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_kstyw_form_warform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:macroform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_kstyw_form_macroform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:microform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_kstyw_form_microform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:titaniform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_kstyw_form_titaniform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:swarmform = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_kstyw_form_swarmform_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mooncat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mooncat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

# Wraith Shapeshift
POD_wraith_shapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_wraith
	icon = POD_wraith_interaction
	desc = POD_wraith_shapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	cooldown = { months = 1 }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = { has_trait = wraith }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { scope:actor = scope:recipient }
			scope:actor = { has_perk = sculpt_perk }
		}
		trigger_else = {
			scope:recipient = { has_trait = wraith }
			scope:actor = { has_perk = imitate_perk }
		}
		scope:actor = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { has_trait = spectre }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = angst0_modifier }
			}
			trigger_else = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = pathos0_modifier }
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { scope:actor = scope:recipient } }
			scope:recipient = { has_relation_necromancer = scope:actor }
		}
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } } # Wraiths can't be possessed but whatever, futureproofing can't hurt
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_variable = original_face }
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = scope:recipient
		}
		flag = restore_face
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_restore_face
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			NOT = { scope:actor = scope:recipient }
		}
		flag = copy_face
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_copy_face
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = sculpt_perk
			}
		}
		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_reshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				has_perk = sculpt_perk
			}
		}
		flag = revertvicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_revertreshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = sculpt_perk
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				is_immortal = yes
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = scope:recipient
			scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
			scope:actor = { has_perk = bodyshape_perk }
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = POD_will_over_form_interaction.physical_trait.tt
					has_variable_list = known_true_name
				}
				POD_reweaving_is_valid_showing_failures_only = yes
			}
		}
		flag = physical_trait
		localization = TRAIT_CATEGORY_PHYSICAL
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:humanoid = yes
			}
		}
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:immortal_age = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:copy_face = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = original_face } }
						POD_create_dummyclone_effect = { CHARACTER = scope:actor }
						if = {
							limit = { exists = scope:dummyclone }
							scope:dummyclone = { copy_inheritable_appearance_from = scope:actor }
							set_variable = {
								name = original_face
								value = scope:dummyclone
							}
						}
						else = {
							send_interface_toast = {
								title = POD_kueijin_spy.duel.failure
								left_icon = scope:actor
								right_icon = scope:recipient
							}
						}
					}
				}
				copy_inheritable_appearance_from = scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:restore_face = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				copy_inheritable_appearance_from = var:original_face
				remove_variable = original_face
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:limb_weapon = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_limb_weapon_modifier
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:skin_armor = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_skin_armor_modifier
			}
			scope:actor = {
				POD_arcanoi_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:physical_trait = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = reweaving_recipient
					value = scope:recipient
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = reweaving_ability
					value = flag:numina_sorcery
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = physical_trait_only
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		#modifier = {
		#	add = 20
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:limb_weapon = yes
		#		scope:skin_armor = yes
		#	}
		#	is_knight = yes
		#	OR = {
		#		is_at_war = yes
		#		trigger_if = {
		#			limit = { is_independent_ruler = no }
		#			liege = { is_at_war = yes }
		#		}
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:humanoid = yes
		#	NOT = { any_scheme = { exists = yes } }
		#	NOT = { any_targeting_scheme = { hostile_scheme_trigger = yes } }
		#	is_at_war = no
		#}
	}
}

	cost = { piety = minor_piety_value }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = { has_trait = lament }
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_perk = familiar_perk
				has_perk = oblivions_husk_perk
			}
		}
	}

	}

			}

			}
			scope:actor = {

					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}

		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes

				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes

			}
			scope:actor = {

			}
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #

			}
			scope:recipient = {

			}
		}
	}

	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

		base = 0

		#modifier = {
		#	add = 20

		#		scope:limb_weapon = yes
		#		scope:skin_armor = yes
		#	}

# Possessed Shapeshift
POD_possessed_shapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_possessed
	icon = POD_fomori_interaction
	desc = POD_possessed_shapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	cooldown = { months = 1 }
	cost = { piety = minor_piety_value }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = { POD_is_possessed_trigger = yes }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_natural_weaponry_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:humanoid = yes
			}
		}
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:immortal_age = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701
			}
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:poison_tumors = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_poison_tumors_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:wolf = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_wolf_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:boar = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_boar_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lion = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_lion_modifier
			}
		}
		# Medium Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:body_expansion = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_body_expansion_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:natural_weaponry = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_show_natural_weaponry_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:fire = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_fire_modifier
			}
		}
		# High Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:bestial_mutation = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_bestial_mutation_modifier
			}
		}
		# Low Intrige Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:shadow = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_shadow_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:giantserpent = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_giantserpent_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_rat_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:bat = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_bat_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:insect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_insect_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:snake = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_snake_modifier
			}
		}
		# High Intrige Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mist = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_mist_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:invisible = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:blood = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_blood_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:swarm = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_swarm_modifier
			}
		}
	}

# Fae Shapeshift
POD_fae_shapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_fae
	icon = POD_metamorphosis_interaction
	desc = POD_fae_shapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading
	use_diplomatic_range = no

	cooldown_against_recipient = { months = realm_time_monthly_cooldown }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = { has_POD_fae_blood_trigger = yes }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = ritual_stone_of_the_true_form_modifier }
		}
		scope:actor = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = glamour0_modifier } }
		realm_actor = yes
		realm_fae = yes
		realm_scene = yes
		scope:recipient = {
			NOT = { has_trait = torpor }
			custom_description = { text = POD_do_not_disturb_text NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_do_not_disturb_flag } }
		}
		POD_cross_umbra_interaction_valid_trigger = yes
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

		is_shown = {

				has_perk = impersonate_perk

	send_option = {
