	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
			POD_cantrip_cost_effect = { COUNT = 1 }
		}
		scope:recipient = {	POD_shapeshift_revert_all_effect = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:humanoid = yes
			}
		}
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:immortal_age = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:copy_face = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = original_face } }
						POD_create_dummyclone_effect = { CHARACTER = scope:actor }
						if = {
							limit = { exists = scope:dummyclone }
							scope:dummyclone = { copy_inheritable_appearance_from = scope:actor }
							set_variable = {
								name = original_face
								value = scope:dummyclone
							}
						}
						else = {
							send_interface_toast = {
								title = POD_kueijin_spy.duel.failure
								left_icon = scope:actor
								right_icon = scope:recipient
							}
						}
					}
				}
				copy_inheritable_appearance_from = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:paste_face = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				copy_inheritable_appearance_from = scope:actor
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = original_face } }
						create_character = {
							age = scope:recipient.effective_age
							faith = scope:recipient.faith
							gender = scope:recipient
							random_traits = no
							dynasty = none
							employer = character:POD_wraith_01
							save_scope_as = new_created_character
						}
						set_variable = {
							name = original_face
							value = scope:new_created_character
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:restore_face = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				copy_inheritable_appearance_from = var:original_face
				remove_variable = original_face
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:restore_face_recipient = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				copy_inheritable_appearance_from = var:original_face
				remove_variable = original_face
			}
		}
		# Low Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:wolf = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_wolf_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:boar = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_boar_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lion = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_lion_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# Medium Prowess Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:giant = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_giant_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:lilliputian = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_lilliputian_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:metal = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_metal_modifier

		if = {

				scope:fire = yes

			scope:recipient = {

		if = {
			limit = {

			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_stress = 50
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {

			limit = {

			}
			scope:actor = {

			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_phoenix_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:unicorn = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_stress = 50
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_unicorn_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:griffin = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_stress = 50
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_griffin_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# Low Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:giantserpent = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_giantserpent_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rat = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_rat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:bat = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_bat_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:insect = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_insect_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:snake = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_snake_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		# High Intrigue Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:blood = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_fae_character_modifier = {
					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_blood_modifier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:mist = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {

			}
			scope:recipient = {

					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier

			}
		}
		# Ultimate Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:celestial_dragon = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {

			}

					MODIFIER = POD_shapeshift_form_celestial_dragon_modifier
				}
			}
		}

		# Pooka Animal Mien #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:pooka_animal_mien = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_show_pooka_animal_mien_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

# Numina

# Sorcery
POD_numina_shapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_mage
	icon = POD_sorcery_interaction
	desc = POD_numina_shapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	cooldown = { months = 1 }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_1_perk }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = ritual_stone_of_the_true_form_modifier }
		}
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_1_perk } }
		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_reshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks } }
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_1_perk } }
		flag = revertvicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_revertreshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { is_immortal = yes } scope:recipient = { is_immortal = yes } }
		is_valid = { scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier } } scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_2_perk } }
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_3_perk } }
		flag = giant
		localization = POD_numina_shapeshift_giant
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_3_perk } }
		flag = dwarf
		localization = POD_numina_shapeshift_dwarf
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_4_perk } }
		flag = rat
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_rat
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_4_perk } }
		flag = bat
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_bat
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_4_perk } }
		flag = snake
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_snake
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_5_perk } }
		flag = wolf
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_wolf
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_5_perk } }
		flag = boar
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_boar
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_5_perk } }
		flag = lion
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_lion
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_5_perk } }
		flag = giantserpent
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_giantserpent
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = {
			scope:actor = {
				POD_reweaving_is_valid_showing_failures_only = yes
			}
			scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_6_perk }
		}
		flag = physical_trait
		localization = POD_numina_shapeshift_physical_trait
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_6_perk }
		}
		flag = dragon
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_dragon_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_6_perk }
		}
		flag = phoenix
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_phoenix_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_6_perk }
		}
		flag = unicorn
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_unicorn_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_6_perk }
		}
		flag = griffin
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_griffin_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_perk = numina_shapeshifting_6_perk }
		}
		flag = celestial_dragon
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_celestial_dragon_modifier
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = { POD_static_stress_gain = { COUNT = 50 } }
		scope:recipient = { POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes remove_trait = giant remove_trait = dwarf }
		if = { limit = { scope:humanoid = yes } }
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = { scope:vicissitudeface = yes }
			scope:recipient = { add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks }
			scope:actor = { set_variable = { name = vicissitude_target value = scope:recipient } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes }
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { scope:immortal_age = yes } scope:actor = { trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701 } }

		if = { limit = { scope:giant = yes } scope:recipient = { add_trait = giant } }
		if = { limit = { scope:dwarf = yes } scope:recipient = { add_trait = dwarf } }

		if = { limit = { scope:rat = yes } scope:actor = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_rat_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:bat = yes } scope:actor = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_bat_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:snake = yes } scope:actor = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_snake_modifier } }

		if = { limit = { scope:wolf = yes } scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_wolf_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:boar = yes } scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_boar_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:lion = yes } scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_lion_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:giantserpent = yes } scope:actor = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_giantserpent_modifier } }

		if = { limit = { scope:dragon = yes } scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_dragon_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:phoenix = yes } scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_phoenix_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:unicorn = yes } scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_unicorn_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:griffin = yes } scope:actor = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_griffin_modifier } }
		if = { limit = { scope:celestial_dragon = yes } scope:actor = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_celestial_dragon_modifier } }

		if = { limit = { scope:physical_trait = yes }
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = { name = reweaving_recipient value = scope:recipient }
				set_variable = { name = reweaving_ability value = flag:numina_sorcery }
			}
			scope:recipient = { add_character_flag = physical_trait_only }
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	auto_accept = yes

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
	}
}

# Psi
POD_psychic_shapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_psychic
	icon = POD_psi_interaction
	desc = POD_psychic_shapeshift_interaction_desc

		scope:actor = scope:recipient

			OR = {
				has_perk = animal_psi_4_perk
				has_perk = biocontrol_1_perk
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = ritual_stone_of_the_true_form_modifier }
		}
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

		flag = humanoid

		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_reshapeface
	}

	send_option = {

		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = biocontrol_1_perk } }
		flag = revertvicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_revertreshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { is_immortal = yes } scope:recipient = { is_immortal = yes } }
		is_valid = { scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier } } scope:actor = { has_perk = biocontrol_1_perk } }
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age

		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = animal_psi_4_perk } }

		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_lion
	}

	send_option = {

		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_bat

	send_option = {

	}

# Charm Shapeshift
POD_charm_shapeshift_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_umbra
	icon = POD_spirit_interaction
	desc = POD_charm_shapeshift_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	cooldown = { months = 1 }
	cost = { piety = minor_piety_value }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = { has_trait = spirit }
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_perk = invisibility_perk
				has_perk = liquefy_perk
				has_perk = shapeshift_perk
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		# scope:recipient = {
			# trigger_if = {
				# limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				# is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			# }
		# }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = shapeshift_perk
			}
		}
		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_reshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
		}
		flag = revertvicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_revertreshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = shapeshift_perk
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				is_immortal = yes
			}
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier }
			}
		}
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = invisibility_perk
			}
		}
		flag = invisible
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = {
				has_perk = liquefy_perk
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_blood_modifier  }
			}
		}
		flag = blood
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_blood_modifier
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_remove_natural_weaponry_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:humanoid = yes
			}
		}
		# Vicissitude Gui #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:vicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				#POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_randomize_effect = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_variable = {
					name = vicissitude_target
					value = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
				remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:immortal_age = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701
			}
		}
		# High Intrige Shapeshift #
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:invisible = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:blood = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_blood_modifier
			}
		}
	}

	ai_frequency = 0
	#
	#ai_targets = {
	#	ai_recipients = self
	#}

	auto_accept = yes

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0

		#modifier = {
		#	add = 40
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:invisible = yes
		#		scope:blood = yes
		#	}
		#	is_at_war = no
		#	OR = {
		#		any_scheme = { scheme_skill = intrigue }
		#		has_council_position = councillor_spymaster
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 20
		#	OR = {
		#		scope:invisible = yes
		#		scope:blood = yes
		#	}
		#	is_at_war = no
		#	any_targeting_scheme = {
		#		hostile_scheme_trigger = yes
		#	}
		#}
		#
		#modifier = {
		#	add = 30
		#	scope:humanoid = yes
		#	NOT = { any_scheme = { exists = yes } }
		#	NOT = { any_targeting_scheme = { hostile_scheme_trigger = yes } }
		#	is_at_war = no

#KJ
POD_shapeshift_by_chi_interaction = {
	category = interaction_category_vampire
	icon = POD_art_interaction
	desc = POD_shapeshift_by_chi_interaction_desc
	options_heading = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_options_heading

	cooldown = { months = 1 }

	is_shown = {
		scope:actor = scope:recipient
		scope:actor = { has_POD_kj_blood_trigger = yes }
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = ritual_stone_of_the_true_form_modifier }
		}
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } }
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = ten_thousand_forms_perk } }
		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_reshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks } }
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = ten_thousand_forms_perk } }
		flag = revertvicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_revertreshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { is_immortal = yes } scope:recipient = { is_immortal = yes } }
		is_valid = { scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier } } scope:actor = { has_perk = ten_thousand_forms_perk } }
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = white_tiger_corpse_perk } }
		flag = invisible
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = ching_shih_perk } }
		flag = ching_shih
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_ching_shih_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = flow_like_blood_perk } }
		flag = blood
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_blood_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = jade_servant_of_the_august_personage_perk } }
		flag = metal
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_metal_modifier
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = jade_servant_of_the_august_personage_perk } }
		flag = wood
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_wood_modifier
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = jade_servant_of_the_august_personage_perk } }
		flag = water
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_water_modifier
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = jade_servant_of_the_august_personage_perk } }
		flag = fire
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_fire_modifier
	}
	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = jade_servant_of_the_august_personage_perk } }
		flag = earth
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_earth_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { has_perk = demon_shintai_1_perk } }
		flag = demonic_form
		localization = POD_shapeshift_by_chi_interaction_demonic_feature
	}

	send_option = {
		is_valid = { scope:actor = { POD_reweaving_is_valid_showing_failures_only = yes has_perk = ten_thousand_forms_perk } }
		flag = physical_trait
		localization = POD_numina_shapeshift_physical_trait
	}

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = { POD_static_stress_gain = { COUNT = 50 } }
		scope:recipient = {
			POD_shapeshift_remove_demonic_feature_effect = yes
			POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
			switch = {
				trigger = yes
				scope:vicissitudeface = {
					scope:actor = {
						set_variable = { name = vicissitude_target value = scope:recipient }
					}
					add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				}
				scope:revertvicissitudeface = {
					POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes
					remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
				}
				scope:immortal_age = { trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701 }
				scope:invisible = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier }
				scope:ching_shih = {
					if = { limit = { has_perk = form_of_infernal_favor_perk } add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_ching_shih_form_of_infernal_favor_modifier }
					else_if = { limit = { has_perk = corpse_awakening_perk } add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_ching_shih_corpse_awakening_modifier }
					else = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_ching_shih_modifier }
				}
				scope:blood = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_blood_modifier }
				scope:metal = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_metal_modifier }
				scope:wood = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_wood_modifier }
				scope:water = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_water_modifier }
				scope:fire = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_fire_modifier }
				scope:earth = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_earth_modifier }
				scope:demonic_form = { POD_shapeshift_show_demonic_feature_effect = yes }
				scope:physical_trait = {
					scope:actor = {
						set_variable = { name = reweaving_recipient value = scope:recipient }
						set_variable = { name = reweaving_ability value = flag:ten_thousand_forms }
					}
					add_character_flag = physical_trait_only
				}
			}
		}

	}

	ai_frequency = 0

	auto_accept = yes

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
	}
}

		scope:actor = scope:recipient
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		scope:recipient = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_perk = aspect_of_duat_perk } }
				is_in_umbra_trigger = no
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = ritual_stone_of_the_true_form_modifier }
		}
		scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_trait = drone } } # Demons can't be possessed but whatever, futureproofing can't hurt
	}

	send_option = {
		flag = humanoid
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_humanoid
	}

	### ARCANA

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_reshape_flesh_perk } }
		flag = vicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_reshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_reshape_flesh_perk }
			scope:recipient = { has_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks }
		}
		flag = revertvicissitudeface
		localization = POD_vampshapeshift_interaction_revertreshapeface
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_reshape_flesh_perk }
			scope:recipient = { is_immortal = yes }
		}
		is_valid = {
			scope:recipient = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = baba_yagas_curse_modifier } }
		}
		flag = immortal_age
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_immortal_age
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_apocalyptic_form_perk } }
		flag = apocalyptic_angelic
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_apocalyptic_angelic_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_apocalyptic_form_perk } }
		flag = apocalyptic_damned
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_apocalyptic_damned_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_incorporeal_form_perk } }
		flag = incorporeal
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_incorporeal_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_invinsibility_perk } }
		flag = invisible
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_celestial_radience_perk } }
		flag = light
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_light_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = { scope:actor = { has_perk = arcana_shadow_mastery_perk } }
		flag = shadow
		localization = POD_shapeshift_form_shadow_modifier
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { 
				has_perk = arcana_arsenal_of_the_beast_perk
				has_arsenal_of_the_beast_trigger = no
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_claws
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_fangs
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_gaping_maw
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_horns
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_lashing_tail
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_poison
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_venom_spit
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_spines
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_tentacles
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_wings
				}
			}
		}
		flag = arsenal_of_the_beast
		localization = arcana_arsenal_of_the_beast_perk_name
	}

	send_option = {
		is_shown = {
			scope:actor = { 
				has_perk = arcana_arsenal_of_the_beast_perk
				NAND = {
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_claws
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_fangs
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_gaping_maw
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_horns
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_lashing_tail
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_poison
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_venom_spit
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_spines
					has_character_flag = arsenal_of_the_beast_tentacles
				}
			}
		}
		flag = purchase_arsenal_of_the_beast
		localization = POD_shapeshift_interaction_purchase_arsenal_of_the_beast
	}
	########

	on_accept = {
		scope:actor = {
			POD_willpower_rouse_check_effect = yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						scope:revertvicissitudeface ?= yes
						scope:vicissitudeface ?= yes
						scope:immortal_age ?= yes
						scope:purchase_arsenal_of_the_beast ?= yes
					}
				}
				POD_shapeshift_revert_effect = yes
				POD_shapeshift_remove_apocalyptic_form_effect = yes
				POD_shapeshift_remove_arsenal_of_the_beast_effect = yes
			}
		}
		switch = {
			trigger = yes
			scope:humanoid = { }
			scope:revertvicissitudeface = { 
				scope:recipient = { POD_shapeshift_vicissitude_face_revert_effect = yes remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks } 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 } }
			}
			scope:vicissitudeface = { 
				scope:recipient = { add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks } 
				scope:actor = { 
					set_variable = { name = vicissitude_target value = scope:recipient } 
					POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
				}
			}
			scope:immortal_age = { 
				scope:actor = { trigger_event = POD_transmogrify.701 POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 3 } } 
			}
			scope:apocalyptic_angelic = { 
				scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_apocalyptic_angelic_modifier } 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 5 } } 
			}
			scope:apocalyptic_damned = { 
				scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_apocalyptic_damned_modifier } 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 5 } } 
			}
			scope:incorporeal = { 
				scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_incorporeal_modifier } 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 4 } } 
			}
			scope:invisible = { 
				scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_invisible_modifier } 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 4 } } 
			}
			scope:light = { 
				scope:recipient = {
					add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_light_modifier
					POD_achiev_morningstar_check = yes
				} 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 4 } } 
			}
			scope:shadow = { 
				scope:recipient = { add_character_modifier = POD_shapeshift_form_shadow_modifier } 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 4 } } 
			}
			scope:arsenal_of_the_beast = { 
				scope:recipient = { POD_shapeshift_show_arsenal_of_the_beast_effect = yes } 
				scope:actor = { POD_resolve_cost_effect = { COUNT = 4 } } 
			}
			scope:purchase_arsenal_of_the_beast = { 
				scope:actor = { trigger_event = POD_demon_arcana.100 } 
			}
			
		}
	}

	auto_accept = yes

	ai_frequency = 0


	ai_will_do = {
		base = 0
	}
}
